  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是利用超声图像自动识别并精细定位儿童TAT病灶，输入为阴囊超声图像，输出为病灶级检测框与像素级分割掩膜，目标是支撑自动化TAT分析与AI方法评估。

Motivation: TAT临床表现非特异、诊断困难，而儿童急性阴囊急症领域缺乏标准化、可公开获取的数据集，导致相关AI方法难以开发与横向比较。

Method: 构建TAT-Det：来自浙江大学医学院附属儿童医院1,203例患者的2,600张超声图像，每张阳性图像提供病灶级边界框与像素级分割掩膜；标注采用多阶段流程，包括儿科超声专科医师独立标注、共识复核与精修；并在该数据上运行代表性深度学习模型建立检测与语义分割基线。

实验效果: 提供了检测与语义分割基线基准：检测任务中YOLO11m表现最佳，分割任务中RWKV-UNet表现最佳；具体指标数值摘要未报告。

Abstract: Background: Testicular appendage torsion (TAT) is the leading cause of acute scrotal pain in prepubertal children. Yet, its diagnosis remains challenging due to nonspecific clinical manifestations and the absence of standardized datasets for pediatric acute scrotal emergencies.
  Results: We present TAT-Det, an expert-annotated ultrasound dataset specifically designed for TAT. TAT-Det comprises 2,600 ultrasound images from 1,203 patients collected at the Children's Hospital, Zhejiang University School of Medicine. Each TAT-positive image is accompanied by lesion-level bounding boxes for detection and pixel-level segmentation masks for fine-grained delineation. Annotations were produced through a multi-stage workflow involving independent annotation by board-certified pediatric sonographers, consensus review, and refinement. Baseline benchmarks for object detection and semantic segmentation using representative deep learning models are further provided, with YOLO11m achieving the best detection performance and RWKV-UNet the best segmentation performance.
  Conclusions: TAT-Det addresses the lack of publicly available resources for pediatric emergency ultrasound and establishes a foundation for developing and evaluating artificial intelligence methods for automated TAT analysis.

</details>


### [106] [A Computational Model of Continuous Recognition Memory for Natural Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33947)
*Sheng Zhao,Yijin Wang,Bei Xiao,Yuhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对人类连续再认记忆（CRM）的计算模型，用极少参数预测逐试次的人类再认判断并估计图像记忆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是建模人类连续再认记忆（CRM），即在连续呈现的图像中判断当前图像此前是否见过。其应用背景包括让机器视觉系统预测并生成更易被记住的图像，以及辅助记忆障碍诊断与记忆干预开发。模型输入为图像序列，输出为观察者在各试次的"是否见过"判断与图像记忆性预测。

Motivation: 现有工作缺乏对人类连续再认记忆的显式计算建模；而已有的图像记忆性预测方法往往需要数百万参数，参数开销大，且未刻画编码、遗忘与决策等记忆机制。

Method: 模型整合三个关键成分：图像编码、记忆衰退与决策。每张图像被编码为感知空间中一个独立的随机变量；随记忆衰退，该变量在空间中的分布发生漂移；判断时，观察者从已编码图像的分布中采样并与当前图像比较以决定是否"见过"。该机制化建模以共享参数刻画群体行为、以个体参数刻画差异，从而在极少参数下替代大规模参数化方法。

实验效果: 模型能在人类被试间的变异范围内预测CRM任务的逐试次反应；仅需四个共享参数加每名观察者两个参数，即可达到不逊于需数百万参数的已有方法的图像记忆性预测精度。摘要未报告具体数值型基准结果。

Abstract: Recognizing an image we have seen before is a basic function of human visual memory. Understanding human continuous recognition memory (CRM) can help machine vision systems predict which images people are likely to remember and generate more memorable images. It can also inform the diagnosis of memory impairments and the development of interventions to improve memory. This paper proposes the first computational model for CRM. The model captures three key components of continuous recognition memory (CRM): image encoding, memory decay, and decision making. It encodes each image as a distinct random variable in a perceptual space. As the memory of an image fades, the distribution of its random variable drifts within that space. To decide whether the current image has been seen before, the observer retrieves samples from the distributions associated with previously encoded images and compares them with the current image. Our model predicts human observers' trial-by-trial responses in CRM tasks within the variability observed across human participants. Our model is parameter efficient, requiring only four shared parameters and two parameters per observer. It can accurately predict the memorability of an image no worse than prior methods that require millions of parameters.

</details>


### [107] [Where to Approximate: Understanding and Accelerating Looped Multi-View Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33948)
*Siyuan Zhang,Yuhan Chen,Yangyang Ning,Nan Wang,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过成对干预实验分析循环多视图重建中近似误差的注入、传播与输出影响，并据此设计加速方案，在五个重建基准上实现约1.63倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 循环多视图重建通过反复应用共享Transformer块来精化场景特征，目标是降低这些迭代更新的计算成本，同时保持位姿与几何重建精度。

Motivation: 要使更新更廉价，需要了解某一步的近似如何影响后续精化过程，而现有工作缺乏对近似误差注入、跨步传播及其对最终输出影响的分层分析。

Method: 在Déjà View与堆叠重建Transformer中开展成对干预，分离误差注入、传播与输出效应；用尺度调整的状态误差度量与局部雅可比分解区分通道门控与空间误差结构响应；据此提出方法：早期降低空间分辨率并池化key/value，将注意力输出复用与完整注意力交替，并通过轻量自蒸馏适配。

实验效果: 在Déjà View中近似误差比匹配随机扰动衰减更强；等最终范数下近似误差对预测旋转的影响为随机误差的1.55倍；均衡配置在五个基准上比官方16步模型快1.63倍，平均位姿AUC@3°仅降1.86点；同延迟下比全精化块基线插值曲线高4.73点AUC@3°，并改善几何指标。

Abstract: Looped multi-view reconstruction refines scene features by repeatedly applying a shared transformer block. Making these updates cheaper requires knowing how an approximation at one step affects the rest of the refinement. We study this with paired interventions in Déjà View and stacked reconstruction transformers, separating the error an approximation injects, how that error propagates, and how it affects the output. In Déjà View, approximation errors are attenuated more strongly than matched random perturbations under a scale-adjusted state-error measure, and a local Jacobian decomposition separates the contribution of channel gating from the response to spatial error structure. Spatial approximations made early benefit from subsequent refinement, whereas cross-step reuse depends on which quantity is cached and how reuse steps are spaced. At equal final norm, approximation errors change the predicted rotations 1.55 times as much as random errors. These findings guide our proposed method, which reduces spatial resolution and pools keys and values early in refinement, then interleaves attention-output reuse with full attention, and is adapted through lightweight self-distillation. Across five reconstruction benchmarks, the balanced configuration runs $1.63\times$ faster than the official 16-step Déjà View model with a 1.86-point reduction in average pose AUC@$3^\circ$. At matched latency, it exceeds the interpolated curve of a trained baseline using full refinement blocks by 4.73 points in AUC@$3^\circ$ and improves the corresponding geometry metrics.

</details>


### [108] [RefMix: Expanding Endpoint Proposals for Diversity-Preserving Reward Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33957)
*Jaemin Kim,Christian Simon,Dongseok Shim,Wei-Yao Wang,Qiyu Wu,Masato Ishii,Shusuke Takahashi,Jong Chul Ye,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RefMix 通过在训练端点生成阶段注入参考模型提供的替代候选，缓解在线前向匹配的在策略反馈循环所导致的视觉多样性退化，同时保持任务奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该论文面向基于流的生成模型的在线前向匹配对齐任务：通过反复生成、奖励、拟合模型产出的端点来优化模型，目标是在保持任务奖励的同时维持生成结果的视觉多样性。

Motivation: 现有在线前向匹配形成在策略反馈循环：高奖励模态被不断强化，其他模态在后续训练池中出现概率下降，视觉多样性逐步衰减；而仅靠推理阶段的参考辅助无法解决训练时端点分布单一的问题。

Method: 提出 RefMix 参考辅助端点提议策略：在早期高噪声阶段引入参考模型以暴露替代视觉模态，随后由当前奖励微调模型完成端点补全，从而将替代候选注入训练池；同时保留原始任务奖励与前向匹配目标不变，可即插即用地接入现有在线前向匹配方法。

实验效果: 在多个最先进在线前向匹配方法上，RefMix 均在保持任务奖励的同时提升视觉多样性；受控推理实验表明训练期端点暴露对改善奖励—多样性权衡至关重要；在可比奖励水平下多样性较基线最高提升 44%。

Abstract: Online forward matching aligns flow-based generative models by repeatedly generating, rewarding, and fitting model-produced endpoints. However, this behavior creates an on-policy feedback loop: as high-reward modes become increasingly favored, alternative modes become less likely to appear in subsequent training pools, progressively reducing visual diversity. We introduce RefMix, a reference-assisted endpoint proposal strategy that addresses this limitation by injecting alternative candidates into the training pool while retaining the original task reward and forward matching objective. In particular, RefMix uses a reference model during early high-noise steps to expose alternative visual modes, followed by the current reward-tuned model for endpoint completion. Across state-of-the-art online forward matching methods, RefMix consistently improves visual diversity while preserving task reward performance. Controlled inference experiments further demonstrate that training-time endpoint exposure is critical to improve reward--diversity trade-offs, beyond the impact of reference-assisted inference. By rethinking how training endpoints are proposed, RefMix achieves up to 44% higher diversity over forward matching baselines at comparable reward levels.

</details>


### [109] [Where Does Free-Text 3D Grounding Fail? An Error Analysis of Report-Sentence Segmentation in Chest CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33958)
*Abdullah Arif Sheikh,Kamalaker Dadi,Raju S. Bapi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对VoxTell模型在ReXGroundingCT验证集全部381条finding上的逐例失败分析表明，其错误主要源于范围估计过大、小病灶漏检和弥漫性病变，而非解剖定位混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 自由文本3D grounding任务要求依据放射报告中的一句话，在CT体数据中分割出该句描述的区域，从而在规模上把报告与影像关联起来；本文目标是厘清现有模型究竟在哪里失败。

Motivation: 现有工作只报告整体性能，尚不清楚当前模型的具体失效模式，因而缺乏对后续改进方向的实证依据。

Method: 对公开的VoxTell模型在ReXGroundingCT验证集（200例扫描、381条finding）上做全覆盖逐例误差分析，统计漏检、错配与过度预测等类型，并开展消融：去除位置词、仅标签提示、以及由句子推导的解剖规则后处理，并在隐藏的300例扫描测试集上验证结论是否一致。

实验效果: VoxTell grounding成功率为53.5%；失败几乎不涉及病灶或侧别混淆（无漏检落在语句所指的对侧），而是预测范围过大、落在同侧邻近病灶、小病灶缺失、弥漫性病变难以grounding；去除位置词使性能减半至仅标签提示的水平，解剖规则后处理从未提升完整句子输出，隐藏测试集上模式一致。

Abstract: Free-text 3D grounding, segmenting in a CT volume the region described by one sentence of the radiology report, could link reports to images at scale, but it is unclear where current models fail. We analyse the publicly released VoxTell model on all 381 findings of the 200-scan ReXGroundingCT validation set, where it grounds 53.5% of findings. Its failures are rarely about which lesion or side is meant. No miss lies on the side opposite to the one named, and findings are almost never confused with one another. Instead, predictions are too large, land on a neighbouring lesion on the correct side, or are missing for small findings, and diffuse findings are rarely grounded. Removing location words halves performance, to the level of label-only prompting, and anatomical rules derived from the sentence never improve the full-sentence output. The same pattern holds on the hidden 300-scan test set. Further progress therefore depends on extent estimation and small-lesion sensitivity rather than on anatomical post-processing.

</details>


### [110] [Embodied-VGGT: Preserving Reliable Local Geometry for Long-Sequence Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33964)
*Yiheng Han,Ziang Chen,Zhuyong Fu,Yiqing Yin,Nan Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Embodied-VGGT 利用具身采集中的平台—环境几何与重复观测线索，在全局优化中构建并选择性地保留可靠局部几何关系，从而抑制长序列重建的漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 面向机器人与自动驾驶的长序列三维重建，需要在全局精化的同时纠正累积漂移。输入为具身采集的连续观测（场景结构与平台运动），输出为保持局部几何一致性的长序列重建轨迹与几何结果，目标是降低轨迹与几何误差。

Motivation: 现有全局优化（如基于全局上下文记忆的方法）在长序列中易引入漂移并破坏原本可靠的局部几何，缺乏对可靠局部关系的显式约束与选择性保留机制。

Method: 提出三个协同组件：MAGC 在 Scal3R 全局上下文记忆基础上，用预测地面几何导出的相对尺度参考正则化测试时记忆更新，提升跨块一致性并限制对可靠局部几何的改动；SA 在空间区域上平衡地面与非地面证据以估计块间平移；CL 利用交叠窗口间的几何与运动一致性筛选连贯块组，对每组施加共享 Sim(3) 修正，在全局优化中保持组内相对变换。

实验效果: 在五个公开驾驶基准上取得一致且显著的提升；KITTI Odometry 上平均绝对轨迹误差 7.30 m、Chamfer 距离 2.67 m，相同评测协议下较当前最优基线分别降低 46.8% 与 23.3%。

Abstract: Long sequence reconstruction must correct accumulated drift while preserving locally consistent geometry. In robotics and autonomous driving, embodied acquisition provides recurring platform- environment geometry and repeated observations of scene structure and platform motion. We present Embodied-VGGT, which exploits these cues to establish reliable local relationships and selectively preserve them during global refinement. Memory-Augmented Grounded Chunks (MAGC) builds on Scal3R's global context memory and uses relative scale references derived from predicted ground geometry to regularize test-time memory updates, encouraging cross-chunk consistency while limiting changes to reliable local geometry. Superpoint Alignment (SA) and Cross-Loop Optimization (CL) jointly address the robust establishment and preservation of geometric relationships. SA balances ground and non-ground evidence across spatial regions to estimate inter-chunk translation. CL uses geometric and motion consistency across interleaved windows to select coherent chunk groups, each receiving a shared Sim(3) correction that preserves its internal relative transformations during global optimization. Experiments on five public driving benchmarks demonstrate consistent and substantial improvements in long sequence reconstruction. On KITTI Odometry, Embodied-VGGT achieves a mean absolute trajectory error of 7.30 m and a Chamfer distance of 2.67 m, reducing these errors by 46.8% and 23.3% compared with the state-of-the-art baseline under same evaluation protocol. To facilitate reproducibility, we released our code: https://github.com/Ang-Sheep/Embodied-VGGT.

</details>


### [111] [FasterGRN: Reducing the Refinement Redundancy for Accelerating Generative Refinement Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33982)
*Ziqian Zhang,Pengcheng Wang,Yulong Chen,Yaxing Wang,Senmao Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的 FasterGRN，通过阶段划分、Adaptive Retention Expansion 与 Selective Token Freezing 削减 GRN 的细化冗余，实现约 2.03 倍采样加速且基本保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: Generative Refinement Networks (GRN) 通过在整个视觉表示上反复迭代细化来生成高质量图像，但该过程带来巨大的计算开销。本文任务是在不重新训练的前提下加速 GRN 推理，输入为其迭代细化生成流程，输出为高质量图像，目标是在保持生成质量的同时显著提升采样效率。

Motivation: GRN 的二值表示与全局细化机制使面向扩散模型和自回归模型的既有加速策略无法直接套用，因此需要针对 GRN 自身生成特性设计的加速方案。

Method: 作者先分析整个生成过程并观察到阶段特性、双分支相似性以及关键的“细化冗余”等性质。据此将 GRN 划分为语义建立阶段与细节细化阶段；针对主要源自细节细化阶段的冗余，提出 Adaptive Retention Expansion（ARE）压缩细化步数，以及 Selective Token Freezing（STF）减少 token 计算量。

实验效果: 在 GenEval 与 DPG-Bench 上实现 2.03× 采样加速且基本保持生成性能；在 COCO 数据集上的评测显示其在图像保真度上甚至优于原始 GRN。

Abstract: Departing from the paradigm of diffusion and autoregressive models, Generative Refinement Networks (GRN) enables high-quality image generation through iterative refinements. However, this repeated processing over the entire visual representations results in substantial computation overhead. Furthermore, the binary representations and global refinement mechanism in GRN disable the direct application of acceleration strategies developed for other generative models. To accelerate GRN model, we first observe several key properties by analyzing the entire generative process. That includes the stage characteristic, dual branch similarity and more importantly, the $\textbf{refinement redundancy}$. Motivated by the observations, we present $\textbf{FasterGRN}$, a training-free acceleration method for the Generative Refinement Network. $\textbf{FasterGRN}$ first divides the GRN into the semantic establishment stage and detail refinement stage according to its semantic establishment. For alleviating the refinement redundancy mainly resulting from the detail refinement stage, we propose Adaptive Retention Expansion ($\textbf{ARE}$) and Selective Token Freezing ($\textbf{STF}$) to shrink refinement steps and reduce token computations, respectively. In the experiments on GenEval and DPG-Bench benchmarks, our method $\textbf{FasterGRN}$ achieves a $2.03\times$ sampling speedup while largely preserving image generation performance. Our evaluation on COCO datasets additionally demonstrates that $\textbf{FasterGRN}$ even outperforms the original GRN in image fidelity.

</details>


### [112] [geo-eval-harness: An Evaluation Harness for Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33990)
*Nils Lehmann,Caleb Robinson,Isaac Corley,Jennifer Marcus,Inbal Becker-Reshef,Juan M. Lavista Ferres,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出开源框架 geo-eval-harness，在统一受控设置下评测 100 个地理空间与通用视觉模型，揭示评估选择对结论的影响并强调需同时报告不确定性与部署算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 地理空间基础模型（GFM）研究需要公平的性能比较，输入为冻结或低秩适配编码器上的多种探针，输出为在分类与分割数据集上的评测结果，目标是分离模型性能与评估方式本身的影响。

Motivation: 现有 GFM 研究采用不一致的评估设置（如不同的预处理和探针），导致无法区分性能提升究竟来自模型本身还是评估选择，因而缺乏可比、可复现的结论。

Method: 构建开源受控对比框架 geo-eval-harness，在 6 个分类和 14 个分割数据集上，用多种探针（冻结或低秩适配编码器）评测 100 个 GFM 与通用视觉模型；并进一步将推理计算量折算为大范围制图工作负载成本，同时报告置信区间与 FLOPs。

实验效果: 受控评估下通用视觉骨干在 RGB 分类与高分辨率分割上具竞争力，GFM 在多光谱任务上更优；前十分类模型两两比较时 95% 置信区间重合率达 73%，更大模型并非总是更好（推理 FLOPs 与分割性能弱相关），在 SpaceNet2 上 0.9 点 mIoU 差异对应 5.9 倍算力与数天额外 GPU 时间。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation model (GFM) research papers are plagued by inconsistent evaluation settings (e.g., different pre-processing and probes), making it difficult to separate performance from evaluation choices. We propose geo-eval-harness}, an open-source framework for controlled model comparisons. We use this harness to evaluate 100 GFMs and general vision models on 6 classification and 14 segmentation datasets, using multiple probes on frozen or low-rank-adapted encoders. We show that under controlled evaluation, general vision backbones are competitive on RGB classification and high-resolution segmentation, but GFMs shine on multispectral tasks. When we compare any two of the top-ten classification models on a single dataset, their 95% confidence intervals overlap 73% of the time. We show that bigger models are not always better: inference FLOPs correlate weakly with segmentation performance. Moreover, translating inference compute into large-area mapping workloads shows how small accuracy differences have outsized deployment cost differences: with frozen encoders on SpaceNet2, a 0.9-point mIoU difference corresponds to 5.9x the compute and days of additional projected GPU time. Our findings stress the importance of comparing models under explicit and fixed settings, and reporting uncertainty and deployment compute alongside accuracy. Our geo-eval-harness makes controlled experiments easy, fast, and fully reproducible. We release the code, configurations, and results at https://github.com/torchgeo/geo-eval-harness.

</details>


### [113] [ScaleCurve: From Constant Scale to Consistent Motion for World-Space Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34005)
*Sanghyeon Lee,Jong Taek Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScaleCurve 利用人体-地面几何估计沿相机路径时变的尺度校正，修复世界坐标人体网格恢复中的局部尺度畸变，并取得 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是在世界坐标系中恢复人体网格：输入为含人体的视频序列及相机轨迹（相机空间人体估计），输出经尺度校准的相机轨迹与提升到世界空间的人体运动，目标是保持空间一致性（全局尺度与局部尺度均正确）。

Motivation: 现有方法即便相机空间人体估计准确，世界空间恢复仍可能出现空间不一致；单一序列全局尺度虽能解决全局尺度歧义，却无法校正沿重建相机路径的局部尺度畸变。

Method: 通过比较预测脚部几何与局部拟合的地面平面构建尺度观测；联合时序优化对不一致观测降权并耦合相邻尺度估计，使校准可随路径自适应而不盲目跟随测量噪声；随后缩放相机平移增量并积分得到校准轨迹；固定该相机，结合 2D 图像证据与预训练 3D 动态先验细化提升后的人体运动，同时限制全局轨迹重缩放。

实验效果: 在 EMDB-2 与 SLOPER4D 上多数评测指标达到 SOTA，且在三种 HMR 骨干上均取得一致增益，说明无需重训练即可兼容不同人体估计；摘要未给出具体数值。

Abstract: World-space human mesh recovery can lose spatial consistency even when camera-space body estimates are accurate. A single sequence-wide scale resolves global scale ambiguity but cannot correct local scale distortion along a reconstructed camera path. We present ScaleCurve, a framework that estimates time-varying metric corrections from human-ground geometry. We construct scale observations by comparing predicted foot geometry with locally fitted ground planes. Joint temporal optimization downweights inconsistent observations and couples neighboring scale estimates, allowing calibration to adapt along the camera path without freely following measurement noise. We scale camera translation increments and integrate them into a calibrated trajectory. With this camera fixed, we refine the lifted body motion using 2D image evidence and pretrained 3D dynamics, while restricting global trajectory rescaling. ScaleCurve achieves state-of-the-art performance on most evaluated metrics on both EMDB-2 and SLOPER4D. Consistent gains across three HMR backbones demonstrate its compatibility with diverse body estimates without retraining. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [114] [Manga Page Parser: Comprehensive Visual Parsing through Local Story Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34006)
*Yingxuan Li,Jiafeng Mao,Arata Furukawa,Mayu Otani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出无需训练的多模态智能体框架 MangaParser，在共享对话中联合完成漫画跨页的角色聚类、说话人分离与说话人-角色绑定，并生成可复用的结构化故事表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 研究任务是漫画视觉解析中的"局部故事理解"：以单个双页跨页（double-page spread）为输入单元，独立分析并组织"谁出现、谁说话"与事件、角色状态及关系，输出可复用的结构化故事表示，目标是为漫画翻译、内容检索与问答提供支撑。

Motivation: 漫画理解需要跨分镜关联角色身份、说话人与其叙事语境，而现有漫画专用模型难以在同一框架内统一处理角色与说话人信息，且通常依赖额外训练或微调，缺乏可跨任务复用的结构化输出。

Method: MangaParser 采用智能体式阅读（agentic reading）框架，在单一多模态对话中联合执行角色聚类、说话人分离和说话人-角色绑定，无需训练或微调；借助结构化故事记忆指导角色与说话人识别，在不确定时检索相关图像区域，并随证据累积同步迭代预测与故事记录，从而在统一表示中关联预测、叙事内容与支撑证据。

实验效果: 定量评测显示，该解析器在多个骨干模型上大幅优于漫画专用模型；基于提问前已生成输出的故事问答实验进一步验证了输出的可复用性。摘要未报告具体基准数值。

Abstract: Understanding manga requires connecting who appears and who speaks with the events, character states, and relationships that unfold across panels. We study manga visual parsing through local story understanding: analyzing each double-page spread independently to capture and organize its story information in a reusable structured representation. We propose MangaParser, an agentic reading framework that jointly performs character clustering, speaker diarization, and speaker-to-character binding within a shared multimodal conversation, without additional training or fine-tuning. The agent uses structured story memory to guide character and speaker identification, inspecting relevant image regions when uncertain and refining its predictions and story records together as evidence accumulates. Quantitative evaluations show that our parser substantially outperforms manga-specific models across multiple backbones. Parsing examples illustrate how a unified representation links the character and speaker predictions with narrative content and supporting evidence. We further test reusability through story question answering using outputs generated before the questions are provided. The results confirm output reusability, offering a foundation for manga translation, content retrieval, and question answering.

</details>


### [115] [Rethinking Unsupervised Cross-Modal Homography Estimation:Spatially Aware Representations and Geometric Consistency Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34021)
*Zhuoran Yang,Junchen Yu,Runmin Zhang,Linshan Li,Chenghao Zhang,Hui-liang Shen,Si-Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoCo 提出几何感知与一致性约束的无监督跨模态单应性估计框架，并通过渐进蒸馏得到轻量模型 GeoCo-D。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是无监督跨模态单应性估计：在无跨模态变换标签条件下，估计两幅跨模态图像间的单应矩阵，输出为用于图像对齐的几何变换参数，目标是在外观差异显著时仍实现精确对齐。

Motivation: 现有做法将模态转换与单应性估计分离以减少外观差异，但准确对齐仍依赖有效的几何表征，且两个子任务之间缺乏一致性约束。

Method: 提出 GeoCo：其估计器 RVHomo 融合冻结视觉基础模型（VFM）的分层特征与局部 CNN 特征，兼顾结构上下文与空间细节；并引入两项几何一致性约束，分别促使模态转换保持几何结构、使单应预测在合成几何变换下保持一致。进一步通过两阶段渐进蒸馏得到 GeoCo-D：中间模型先学习无需模态转换的跨模态匹配，再将预测与特征知识迁移给学生模型，从而免除在线 VFM 提取。

实验效果: 在 GoogleMap、OPT-SAR、FSD、VIS-IR 上，GeoCo 相较各数据集上最优基线将平均角点误差降低 17.4–46.7%；GeoCo-D 优于所有对比基线，且相比完整教师模型参数量减少 98.5%、FLOPs 减少 70.4%。

Abstract: Unsupervised cross-modal homography estimation is challenging because appearance differences obscure shared geometric structures. Separating modality transfer from homography estimation helps reduce these differences, but accurate alignment still requires effective geometric representations and consistency constraints on the two subtasks. We propose GeoCo (Geometry-Aware and Consistency-Constrained Homography Estimation), a framework that addresses these two requirements without cross-modal transformation labels. Its homography estimator, RVHomo, adapts hierarchical features from a frozen vision foundation model (VFM) and fuses them with local CNN features to capture structural context and spatial detail. Two geometric consistency constraints encourage modality transfer to preserve geometry and homography predictions to remain consistent under synthetic geometric transformations. Through two-stage progressive distillation, we further obtain GeoCo-D, which uses the lightweight RVHomo-D estimator. An intermediate model first learns to perform cross-modal matching without modality transfer, then transfers prediction and feature knowledge to the student, eliminating online VFM extraction. Experiments on GoogleMap, OPT-SAR, FSD, and VIS-IR show that GeoCo reduces mean average corner error by 17.4-46.7% relative to the best-performing baseline on each dataset. GeoCo-D also outperforms all compared baselines while using 98.5% fewer parameters and 70.4% fewer FLOPs than the complete GeoCo teacher.

</details>


### [116] [MoSk: A Unified Motion-Skeleton Foundation Model for Human Motion Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34022)
*Yun Chen,Munchurl Kim,Jeonghyeok Do*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoSk是首个融合大规模运动数据与骨架数据的运动基础模型，通过自监督掩码运动建模学习共享表示，在动作识别与运动生成任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该工作面向人体运动表示学习：目标是利用基础模型的语义丰富表示来服务于运动理解与生成任务，输入为运动数据与多种骨架格式数据，输出为可迁移的共享运动表示，并在动作识别与运动生成上验证其有效性。

Motivation: 现有基础模型在视觉理解与生成上表现优异，但在人体运动领域的作用仍未被充分探索：大型运动数据集规模大、动作多样却缺乏动作标签，难以系统评估学到的表示；骨架数据集虽有成熟识别协议便于评估，但规模较小且局限于受控环境下的预定义动作，二者的优势难以兼得。

Method: MoSk通过自监督掩码运动建模在运动与骨架数据上学习共享表示；引入关节名称嵌入，为不同骨架格式提供统一的解剖学参照；采用交叉注意力解码器，依据关节名称嵌入指定目标关节，从编码器特征重建被掩码的运动，从而在异构骨架格式间实现统一建模。

实验效果: 实验显示在多种设定（线性评估与微调）下动作识别性能强劲；通过表示对齐，MoSk在多个骨干网络上显著改善运动生成，在MotionMillion上将FID最多降低9.75（无条件生成）与3.83（文本到运动生成）。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have advanced visual understanding and generation by providing semantically rich representations. However, this role of FMs remains underexplored in human motion. Large motion datasets offer diverse movements at scale, but often lack action labels for systematically evaluating learned representations on understanding tasks. Skeleton datasets enable such evaluation through established recognition protocols, yet are typically smaller and focus on predefined actions captured in controlled environments. To combine these strengths, we present MoSk, to the best of our knowledge, the first Motion-Skeleton foundation model to integrate motion and skeleton datasets. MoSk learns shared representations through self-supervised masked motion modeling. We incorporate joint-name embeddings to provide a common anatomical reference across various skeleton formats. A cross-attention decoder reconstructs masked motion from the encoder's features, with joint-name embeddings specifying the target joints. Experiments demonstrate strong action-recognition performance across diverse settings, including linear evaluation and fine-tuning. MoSk also substantially improves motion generation across multiple backbones through representation alignment, reducing FID on MotionMillion by up to 9.75 for unconditional generation and 3.83 for text-to-motion generation.

</details>


### [117] [Incorporating the Knowledge Distillation to Improve the EfficientNet Transfer Learning Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34025)
*Tjeng Wawan Cenggoro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 作者提出以知识蒸馏为迁移机制的迁移学习方法，将较大 EfficientNet 架构的知识迁移到较小架构，从而提升各规模模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 在计算机视觉深度学习中，迁移学习通常以 ImageNet 上的先进架构（含 EfficientNet 系列）作为骨干网络；本文任务是研究能否把大 EfficientNet 的知识迁移给小 EfficientNet，并衡量其对模型性能的影响。

Motivation: 现有迁移学习实践普遍只依赖 ImageNet 预训练的骨干网络权重，未利用 EfficientNet 系列"由小到大逐级生长"这一结构关系所带来的潜在知识复用空间，即小模型是否能够承接大模型的知识尚缺乏验证。

Method: 基于 EfficientNet 由基线架构渐进生长而来这一观察，提出假设：较小的 EfficientNet 架构能够容纳较大架构的知识；据此设计一种迁移学习方法，以知识蒸馏作为从大架构到小架构的知识转移机制，并对各规模的 EfficientNet 分别实施该迁移流程。

实验效果: 摘要未报告具体基准、对比数值或量化指标；定性结果表明，所提方法能提升每一个 EfficientNet 架构的性能，且若干较小架构甚至优于使用标准迁移学习方法训练的大架构。

Abstract: Due to the Deep Learning requirement for a large training dataset, Transfer Learning has become a central method in the field of Computer Vision, which heavily used Deep Learning. Since the adoption of transfer learning in the field, the performance of the models in computer vision is significantly upgraded. The common transfer learning practice in computer vision research is to use state-of-the-art architectures in the ImageNet dataset as the backbone network. The best performing architecture in ImageNet is currently held by EfficientNet, which is a set of architectures that were progressively grown from a baseline architecture.
  By observing the EfficientNet development approach, it is natural to hypothesize that the smaller EfficientNet architecture is capable to contain the knowledge of the larger architecture. This hypothesis comes from the fact that the larger EfficientNet architecture was grown from the smaller architecture. Therefore, in this study, we experimented if it is beneficial to transfer the knowledge from the larger architecture to the smaller architecture of the EfficientNet. To achieve this goal, we proposed a transfer learning method that uses Knowledge Distillation as the knowledge transfer mechanism from the larger to smaller architecture. We found that the proposed method is able to upgrade the performance of each of the EfficientNet architecture. Several smaller architectures are even able to outperform the larger architecture that was trained using the standard transfer learning method.

</details>


### [118] [ContextHands: Completing Hand Motion with Learned Context and Joint-Specific Body Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34027)
*Taewoo Kim,Seunghun Koh,Hyosup Shin,Sungho Jo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ContextHands 结合身体序列与文本描述，通过扩散模型生成细节丰富的手部动作，提升生成人体动作的真实感与表现力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务是在给定观测到的身体动作序列、以及分配给手部与时间区间的文本描述条件下，补全手部动作；目标是让手部关节细节与动作语境和语言描述相衔接，从而提升生成人体动作的真实感。

Motivation: 现有研究缺乏从身体动作与语言中补全手部细节的能力，而这一细节依赖于将动作语境与手部关节活动方式相联系，此前的通用人体动作生成方法难以刻画这种联系。

Method: 从文本描述与身体动作中学习概率化的手部特征，并借助辅助手部预测将这些特征与手部关节活动关联；身体编码器在多时间尺度上保留关节与身体部位特征，使每个手部关节能查询关节特定的身体信息；扩散生成器将该关节特定身体信息与所学的手部上下文融合；两个编码器与生成器联合训练，使最终生成目标同时塑造上下文特征。

实验效果: 在 OakInk2 数据集上，ContextHands 生成的手部动作更准确，Fréchet Hand Distance 低于适配该任务的手部动作生成方法与通用人体动作生成方法；在抓取与工具使用的定性示例中，其更接近参考动作的细节手部姿态。

Abstract: Detailed hand motion contributes to the realism and expressiveness of generated human motion. Completing these details from body motion and language requires connecting the context of an action to the articulation of the hands. We introduce ContextHands, which completes hand motion from an observed body sequence and text descriptions assigned to hands and time intervals. Our approach learns probabilistic hand features from the descriptions and body motion, using auxiliary hand prediction to connect these features to hand articulation. A body encoder preserves joint and body-part features across multiple temporal scales, allowing each hand joint to query these features for joint-specific body information. The diffusion generator combines this joint-specific body information with the learned hand context. Both encoders and the generator are trained jointly, so the final generation objective also shapes the context features. Experiments on the OakInk2 dataset show that ContextHands generates more accurate hand motion and achieves lower Fréchet Hand Distance than existing hand-motion and general human-motion generation methods adapted to the task. In qualitative examples of grasping and tool use, ContextHands more closely reproduces the detailed hand poses in the reference motion. We will release our code and pretrained models to support further research.

</details>


### [119] [Counter-DECEIVR: Counterfactual Explanations for Deepfake Detectors using Detector-Guided Rectified Flow Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34032)
*Mariano V. Ntrougkas,Vasileios Mezaris,Ioannis Patras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Counter-DECEIVR，一种无需训练的框架，利用现成深度伪造检测器的梯度引导修正流模型的潜变量，生成反事实样本以解释检测器决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 生成式深度伪造内容日益泛滥，检测器虽被广泛使用但黑箱性强、可信度受限；本任务的目标是为给定检测器与输入图像生成反事实样本，即揭示"最小改动即可翻转判定"的证据，帮助分析者判断是否信任检测结果。

Motivation: 现有解释手段存在两重局限：基于区域的解释随着生成质量提升而愈发失效；已有反事实生成方法又往往需要针对每个样本或每个检测器进行拟合，或在目标数据上训练扩散模型，成本高、通用性差。

Method: Counter-DECEIVR（DEtector-guided reCtifiEd flow InVeRsion for counterfactuals）属无训练框架：在修正流模型的velocity field中，借助现成深度伪造检测器的梯度直接操控潜变量以完成反演与编辑，既不训练生成器也不适配检测器；同时利用检测器的解释图将编辑集中在显著区域，把低层特征重要性转化为局部化反事实改动；编辑强度由单一参数控制。

实验效果: 在两个深度伪造检测器和两个常用取证数据集上，Counter-DECEIVR 优于已有反事实生成方法（包括基于训练的方法）；摘要未报告具体数值指标。

Abstract: Deep-learning-generated media are increasingly passed off as real, driving an arms race between image generators and deepfake detectors. Trust in the latter is limited by their often black-box nature; and, while region-based explanations can sometimes be extracted, these become less useful as generation quality improves. Counterfactual examples, which show the minimal change that flips a detector's decision, reveal what evidence drives a verdict, helping analysts decide whether to trust it. This work introduces Counter-DECEIVR (DEtector-guided reCtifiEd flow InVeRsion for counterfactuals), a novel training-free framework that generates counterfactual examples. Counter-DECEIVR steers latents in the velocity field of a rectified flow model using gradients from off-the-shelf deepfake detectors, without training a generator or adapting the detector; unlike prior methods, it requires no per-instance or per-detector fitting and no diffusion training on the target data. It also leverages the detector's explanation maps to concentrate editing to salient regions, turning low-level feature importance into localised counterfactual edits. A single parameter controls the strength of the edits. Across two deepfake detectors and two widely used forensics datasets, Counter-DECEIVR outperforms prior counterfactual generation methods, including training-based ones. Code will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [120] [Understanding Diffusion Acceleration: Methods, Trade-Offs, and Open Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34056)
*Xiaofeng Mao,Fu-yun Wang,Le Zhuo,Zhengkai Jiang,Shaohao Rui,Weijie Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文以统一记号系统梳理并比较扩散与流式生成模型的四类推理加速方法（蒸馏、一致性训练、采样方法、去噪器设计），并讨论质量—成本权衡与开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 扩散与流式生成模型可高质量合成图像、音频与视频，但采样需数十至数百次去噪器评估，随分辨率、视频长度或模型规模增长而开销加剧，影响交互与边缘应用中的时延与能耗。本文目标是系统比较加速推理的各类途径并明确其质量—成本权衡。

Motivation: 现有加速方法分散在不同框架与记号下，难以直接比较各自改变了学习目标、采样轨迹还是单次去噪器计算量，缺乏统一视角来评估质量与成本的取舍。

Method: 考察四条加速路线：蒸馏、一致性训练、采样方法、去噪器设计；用统一记号表述训练目标、转移映射与数值更新，从而将各方法的变化对应到学习目标、采样轨迹与单次去噪器计算量三个维度上进行比较。

实验效果: 摘要未报告新的实验结果；其证据来自已发表图像与视频基准的归纳，用以说明一步/少步生成的质量—成本权衡、求解器与时间步调度之间的交互、特征缓存的作用，以及首帧时延与持续流式吞吐之间的差异。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based generative models support high-quality image, audio, and video synthesis. Sampling can require dozens to hundreds of denoiser evaluations, with each pass becoming more expensive as resolution, video length, or model size increases. This cost affects latency and energy use in interactive and edge applications. We examine four approaches to accelerating inference: distillation, consistency training, sampling methods, and denoiser design. We express training objectives, transition maps, and numerical updates in a common notation to compare changes to the learning target, the sampling trajectory, and the work performed within each denoiser pass. Published image and video benchmarks illustrate the quality--cost trade-offs of one- and few-step generation, the interaction between solvers and timestep schedules, the effect of feature caching, and the difference between first-frame latency and sustained streaming throughput. We close with open problems in visual-native architectures and post-compression distillation, solver-aware few-step training, and semantic trajectory alignment with factorized distribution matching.

</details>


### [121] [Surf-Style: Surface-Based Scene Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34062)
*Wanrong Hong,Ancheng Lin,Mukesh Prasad,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Surf-Style 通过在三维场景中重建物体表面并在其上定义可学习外观模型，实现了跨视角一致且可局部控制的三维风格化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 面向沉浸式可视化、AR/VR 内容创作与艺术化场景渲染等应用，任务是根据参考图像对三维场景进行风格迁移，输出在各视角下保持一致且可控的风格化渲染结果。

Motivation: 逐视角独立应用 2D 风格化会产生跨视角不一致；基于隐式辐射场的方法虽提升了—致性，但像素外观与可学习场景参数之间仅存在间接依赖，导致颜色更新存在歧义，优化过程中难以施加一致的风格约束。

Method: 先重建场景中的物体表面，再在三维空间上定义可学习外观模型，并依据参考图像的视觉风格对其进行修改；借助表面几何实现几何一致性与局部化风格化，从而规避隐式表示中颜色更新的歧义问题。

实验效果: 摘要未报告定量实验结果与具体基准数值；定性上称相较最先进方法具有更优的一致性，并在可控风格化方面更灵活，可针对特定表面区域并组合多种风格。

Abstract: Consistent and controllable 3D stylization is critical for visual computing applications such as immersive visualization, augmented and virtual reality content creation, and artistic scene rendering. While 2D image stylization has advanced significantly, extending these methods to 3D remains challenging due to the need for cross-view consistency, where stylized renderings must align across all viewpoints. Applying 2D stylization independently to each rendered view often results in inconsistencies across perspectives. Existing methods attempt to improve consistency by leveraging implicit radiance field representations, which model scene appearance as a function of spatial locations. However, these methods struggle with ambiguity in updating colors, as the indirect dependence between pixel appearance and learnable scene parameters makes it difficult to enforce consistent stylization during optimization. This work presents Surface-based Scene Stylization (Surf-Style), which reconstructs object surfaces in a 3D scene and uses these surfaces to achieve geometric consistency and localized stylization. Surf-Style defines a learnable appearance model over 3D space, which is modified to replicate the visual style of a reference image. Compared to state-of-the-art methods, our method achieves superior consistency and offers greater flexibility in controllable stylization, including the ability to target specific surface regions and combine different styles.

</details>


### [122] [SPARC: Geometry-Guided Attention Pruning for Long-Horizon Camera-Controlled Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34064)
*Zesong Yang,Weikai Chen,Liyuan Cui,Yuan Li,Jiaxin Huang,Lutao Jiang,Runze Zhang,Yingda Yin,Xiaoyang Huang,Kai Yan,Keyang Luo,Xin Wang,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPARC是一个几何引导的注意力剪枝框架，将预训练视频扩散Transformer的原生自注意力同时用作相机控制与持久场景记忆机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务面向基于预训练视频扩散Transformer的可控视频生成：输入为历史观测场景与新相机轨迹，输出为相机遵循且场景一致的视频，目标是同时实现精确相机控制与场景重访一致性。

Motivation: 现有方法需引入额外的可学习控制模块或记忆模块来实现相机控制与历史检索，本文则指出注意力本身的连通性即可同时承担几何条件化与空间寻址功能，从而避免额外模块开销。

Method: 基于重建已观测内容与合成新区域对上下文需求的不对称性，SPARC对已观测token仅保留几何对应的历史token与局部时空上下文，对未观测区域保留稠密注意力；并采用对应关系感知的注意力偏置与顺序无关的历史定位，使长时序间隔下仍能稳定检索，且不引入额外可学习控制或记忆模块。

实验效果: 实验表明激进的注意力剪枝能提升相机遵循度、重访一致性与视觉保真度，结合四步蒸馏后推理加速超过50倍。

Abstract: We present SPARC, a geometry-guided attention pruning framework that turns the native self-attention of pretrained video diffusion transformers into a unified mechanism for camera control and persistent scene memory. Our key insight is that attention connectivity can simultaneously serve as geometric conditioning for camera control and spatial addressing for historical memory retrieval. Motivated by the asymmetric contextual requirements of reconstructing observed content and synthesizing novel regions, SPARC restricts observed tokens to geometrically corresponding historical tokens and local spatiotemporal context, while preserving dense attention for unobserved regions. Correspondence-aware attention biasing and order-invariant historical positioning further enable stable retrieval across long temporal gaps. Without introducing additional learnable control or memory modules, SPARC achieves precise camera adherence and faithful scene revisitation while substantially reducing attention computation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of aggressive attention pruning in improving camera adherence, revisit consistency, and visual fidelity, with an inference speedup of over 50x when combined with four-step distillation. Project page is available at https://zju3dv.github.io/SPARC.

</details>


### [123] [When to Respond, Where to Ground: Streaming 3D Affordance Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34067)
*Wenting Luan,Weilong Yan,Siqi Lu,Ming Li,Beibei Lin,Yongbin Zheng,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamAff 提出一种证据引导的响应策略，在连续 RGB-D 流与语言请求下同时决定何时响应以及定位何处功能部件，并配套发布 StreamFun3D 基准与 StreamIoU 指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务为流式 3D 可供性（affordance）定位：输入是连续 RGB-D 流与语言请求，智能体只能使用目前观测到的信息，输出为响应时机决策与所请求功能部件在 3D 点上的掩码定位，目标是联合优化二者。

Motivation: 现有 3D 可供性定位方法假设场景观测完整，无法用于在线流式场景；而流式视觉-语言模型又缺乏细粒度的 3D 功能部件定位能力，因此时机决策与部件定位这一联合问题尚无合适方案。

Method: StreamAff 利用定位模型自身的视觉证据同时做两个决策：对已读帧的视觉 patch 打分，将累计支持度与"等待"选项比较以决定何时响应，最高分 patch 决定在哪一帧定位；条件式 look-back 策略使模型可在请求时刻基于较早观测作答或等待新证据；zoom-and-refine 定位在所选帧的 3D 点上分割答案所指部件。作者还构建 StreamFun3D 基准（含 Current、History、Future、Unobserved 四种证据设置）与 StreamIoU 指标（仅在响应及时且源帧有效时计入掩码），并对齐离线 3D 可供性方法与外部决策基线。

实验效果: 在 15 秒流上 StreamAff 达到 30.14% StreamIoU，相较最强基线（离线 3D 可供性方法与视觉-语言模型+掩码解码器的外部决策基线）相对提升 40.6%。

Abstract: Given a continuous RGB-D stream and a language request, streaming 3D affordance grounding requires an agent to decide when to respond and where to locate the requested functional part using only observations available so far. Existing 3D affordance grounding methods typically assume complete scene observations, while streaming vision-language models lack fine-grained 3D functional grounding. Our goal is to jointly determine response timing and functional-part localization during continuous scene observation. We propose StreamAff, an evidence-guided response strategy that makes both decisions from the grounding model's own visual evidence. The model scores the visual patches of the frames it reads: their combined support, weighed against a wait option, decides when to respond, and the highest-scoring patch selects the frame to ground in. A conditional look-back policy lets the model answer from an earlier observation at the moment of the request or wait for new evidence, and zoom-and-refine grounding segments the part named in the answer on the 3D points of the selected frame. We further introduce StreamFun3D, a benchmark derived from real-world 3D scenes that evaluates response timing and grounding across four evidence settings: Current, History, Future, and Unobserved, together with StreamIoU, a metric that credits a mask only when the response is timely and grounded in a valid source frame. We adapt offline 3D affordance grounding methods and build external-decider baselines that pair vision-language models with our mask decoder. StreamAff achieves 30.14% StreamIoU on 15-second streams, a 40.6% relative improvement over the strongest of these baselines.

</details>


### [124] [Hijacking 3D Generation: Targeted Adversarial Attacks against Image-to-3D Models via Prefix Trajectory Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34075)
*Yilong Yang,Jiale Teng,Yule Liu,Yang Liu,Xinlei He,Zhuo Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PTA3D，一种针对图生 3D 模型的定向对抗攻击方法，通过前缀轨迹对齐与预处理感知优化，在仅检查可见内容的审查机制下诱导生成受策略限制的 3D 几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 在线图生 3D 服务允许用户由单张图像生成高质量 3D 资产，上传图像需先通过受策略限制的检查器审查。本文的目标是在黑盒条件下，使视觉上无害的输入图像诱导模型生成违反内容策略的 3D 几何。

Motivation: 现有攻击存在两点不足：检查器只看输入图像的可见内容，故存在可利用的“良性图像—违规几何”错配漏洞；但在黑盒场景下，沿长 DiT 生成轨迹全局反传难以拟合，且各服务预处理流程差异显著会削弱迁移性。

Method: 提出两个组件：前缀轨迹对齐（PTA）逐步对齐对抗轨迹与目标轨迹的前缀 DiT 输出，在核心几何结构形成阶段施加控制，避免优化完整生成轨迹；预处理感知优化（PAO）将对抗优化暴露于多种图像变换而非单一预处理流程，降低对代理服务与目标服务间不匹配的敏感度。作者还建立了前缀到输出界与预处理感知迁移界，从理论上刻画两种设计的效果。

实验效果: 在三个开源图生 3D 模型与三个在线服务上验证：在线服务 ASR 达 41.5%–88.5%，白盒设置 ASR 达 90.8%–94.6%，显著优于现有攻击方法。

Abstract: Online services that deploy Image-to-3D models enable users to generate high-quality 3D assets from a single image. However, the generated 3D geometry can violate content policies and cause broader security risks. To prevent such content, these services typically use policy-restricted inspectors to examine uploaded images before generation. Since the inspector examines only the visible content of the input, a visually benign image can still induce the generation of policy-restricted 3D geometry. However, turning this vulnerability into an effective attack is non-trivial in the black-box setting: global backpropagation through the long DiT generation trajectory is difficult to fit, while different image preprocessing pipelines across services can substantially reduce transferability. In this paper, we present PTA3D, a targeted adversarial attack against Image-to-3D models that addresses these challenges through Prefix Trajectory Alignment (PTA) and Preprocessing Aware Optimization (PAO). PTA progressively aligns the prefix DiT outputs of the adversarial trajectory with those of the target trajectory, where the core geometric structure is formed, allowing the final geometry to be controlled without optimizing through the complete generation trajectory. PAO exposes adversarial optimization to diverse image transformations rather than a single preprocessing pipeline, reducing sensitivity to the mismatch between surrogate and target services. We establish a prefix-to-output bound and a preprocessing-aware transfer bound to theoretically characterize the effectiveness of the two designs. Experiments on three open-source Image-to-3D models and three online services show that PTA3D achieves 41.5%-88.5% ASR on online services and 90.8%-94.6% ASR in white box settings, substantially outperforming existing attacks.

</details>


### [125] [Projector Subspace Optimization for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34078)
*Hao Yu,Quan Cheng,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PROUD，一种针对多模态投影器末端线性层的持续子空间优化框架，通过解析构造度量正交子空间并施加精确 Frobenius 正则，实现无回放、零推理开销的多模态持续指令微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 多模态持续指令微调（MCIT）旨在让经指令微调的多模态大模型（MLLM）在连续到达的任务序列上持续学习。输入为依次到来的多模态指令任务，输出为各任务上表现良好且不遗忘历史能力的模型，目标是在稳定-可塑性间取得平衡。

Motivation: 现有无回放方法在多层级解码器中扩展提示或路由 LoRA 专家，带来沉重的参数与路由开销，并改变内部特征分布。作者指出现有低秩子空间方法仅依据历史统计或任务梯度选择方向，难以兼顾可塑性与稳定性，且无约束的多步更新仍会累积较大漂移。

Method: 将视觉-语言投影器的末端线性层视为高容量、坐标平稳的"接口"，其漂移源于历史视觉 token 被挤占。PROUD 在任务开始时结合保留的输入统计与初始梯度，解析地提取在漂移预算下最大化一阶任务增益的度量正交子空间；训练中在该固定子空间内以精确 Frobenius 惩罚约束更新，最后无损合并，推理与路由零开销。

实验效果: 在 UCIT 与 CoIN 基准、多个 MLLM 骨干上，PROUD 在持续准确率上优于近期无回放基线，同时以一小部分可训练参数抑制遗忘；摘要未给出具体数值。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance through instruction tuning, yet adapting them to sequential tasks requires Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT). Existing replay-free methods expand prompts or routed LoRA experts across multi-layer decoders, incurring heavy parameter and routing overhead while shifting internal feature distributions. Instead, we identify the final linear layer of the vision-language projector as a high-capacity, coordinate-stationary bridge, where sequential updates easily induce interface drift by displacing historical visual tokens. While low-rank subspace adaptation offers a compact remedy, selecting directions solely from historical statistics or task gradients sacrifices plasticity or stability, and unconstrained multi-step updates still accumulate large drift. To resolve both issues, we propose Projector Refinement through Optimized Update Directions (PROUD), a continual subspace optimization framework for the terminal projector layer. At task onset, PROUD combines retained input statistics with initial gradients to analytically extract a metric-orthonormal subspace maximizing first-order task gain under a drift budget. During training, it regularizes updates within this fixed subspace via an exact Frobenius penalty before losslessly merging them with zero inference or routing overhead. Experiments on UCIT and CoIN across MLLM backbones show that PROUD outperforms recent replay-free baselines in continual accuracy while curbing forgetting with a fraction of trainable parameters.

</details>


### [126] [WorldBridge: Bridging Scattered Views for Coherent 3D World Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34079)
*Weilong Yan,Jingyu Hu,Hongli Xu,Yifan Zhan,Beibei Lin,Anbang Wang,Qinqian Lei,Jingwei Huang,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldBridge 提出一个从稀疏、低重叠甚至无重叠图像生成连贯可控三维世界的框架，并配套构建了相应评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是从视觉重叠极少或无重叠的离散图像生成连贯、可探索的三维世界；输入为稀疏参考图像，输出为沿目标轨迹一致的三维几何与外观。

Motivation: 现有几何感知方法无法处理无重叠参考；图像/视频类方法虽能生成未观测区域，却导致跨视角几何不一致。且当前基准很少评测稀疏低重叠条件下的世界生成。

Method: 将稀疏参考编码为全局场景上下文以引导跨视角几何与外观生成；引入时空参考注意力，在目标轨迹上增强时序参考保真度并支持轨迹外的空间参考；提出即插即用的潜参考一致性策略，通过重投影引导的测试时优化提升参考保真度；并构建 WorldBridge 基准套件。

实验效果: 摘要未报告具体数值结果；仅给出定性与定量对比结论：方法实现了可控且连贯的三维世界生成，并优于现有最先进方案。

Abstract: Generating coherent and explorable 3D worlds from scattered images with little or no visual overlap is a fundamental and long-standing challenge in computer vision, which existing geometry-aware methods fail to address. Meanwhile, existing image- and video-based methods may generate unseen regions but produce inconsistent geometry across views. To bridge this gap, we present WorldBridge, a framework for coherent and controllable 3D world generation under scattered images with little or no visual overlap. WorldBridge encodes scattered references into a global scene context to guide geometry and appearance generation across views. Our temporal and spatial reference attention mechanism further enhances the fidelity of temporal references along the target trajectory and supports flexible spatial references outside it. A plug-and-play latent-reference consistency strategy improves reference fidelity through reprojection-guided test-time optimization. We also introduce the WorldBridge benchmark suite to evaluate 3D world generation from scattered, low-overlap references, which is rarely explored by current benchmarks. Both qualitative and quantitative results demonstrate that our method enables controllable and coherent 3D world generation, surpassing the existing state-of-the-art solutions. Our project page is available at https://davidyan2001.github.io/WorldBridge/.

</details>


### [127] [TaCo: Rethinking Correlation for Visual Tracking on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34084)
*Qingmao Wei,Fagui Liu,Quan Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TaCo（Target-aligned Correlation），在无注意力机制的前提下补齐相关滤波类跟踪器缺失的目标条件化能力，在边缘NPU与ARM CPU上实现更优的速度-精度折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 视觉目标跟踪任务以视频首帧给定的目标模板为输入，逐帧输出目标在搜索区域中的位置。在低功耗边缘硬件（NPU、ARM CPU）上部署时，实时性与精度的平衡是核心目标。

Motivation: 注意力机制虽取代互相关成为轻量跟踪器主流，但其softmax、归一化与reshape等访存密集型算子在边缘硬件上的实际耗时远超其算术量暗示的开销；而纯相关跟踪器速度快却精度不足，除非改用ViT骨干——这又把注意力带了回来。作者指出相关操作缺少"以模板对搜索特征进行条件化"这一环节，仅在无目标先验的特征上做空间匹配。

Method: 将模板-搜索交互拆解为"条件化"与"匹配"两个角色，并让模板仅以每视频计算一次的操作数形式进入逐帧网络：一是由目标描述子对骨干内搜索特征做逐通道的缩放与平移（特征条件化）；二是构造与目标框对齐的卷积核，其权重由模板预测，通过一次矩阵乘法与搜索特征相关完成匹配，从而在固定骨干下以极低的NPU额外开销替代注意力交互。

实验效果: 在统一数值校验协议下于边缘NPU与ARM CPU上评测：TaCo-T在RV1126B NPU上以184 FPS取得62.3% LaSOT AUC，树莓派5 CPU上达96 FPS，NPU速度分别为HiT-Tiny和AsymTrack-T的3.0倍与3.5倍且精度更高；TaCo-B在NPU上以50 FPS取得65.6%，速度超过HiT-Base、AsymTrack-B和LightFC-vit两倍以上且精度更高。

Abstract: Lightweight trackers have replaced cross-correlation with attention, and on low-power edge hardware they pay for it, since memory-bound softmax, normalization and reshaping operators of attention cost far more time than their arithmetic suggests. Benchmarking efficient trackers on an edge Neural Processing Unit (NPU) and an Arm CPU under one numerically validated protocol, we find that correlation trackers are the fast ones, but they lag in accuracy unless they adopt a ViT backbone, which brings attention back. We revisit what correlation lacks. Template--search interaction serves two roles: conditioning the search features on the target and matching them against it spatially. Attention does both, whereas correlation only matches, and does so on features extracted without knowledge of the target. We present TaCo (Target-aligned Correlation), which supplies the missing conditioning without attention by letting the template enter the per-frame network only as operands computed once per video. A descriptor of the target scales and shifts the search features channel by channel inside the backbone, and kernels aligned with the target box, each with a weight predicted from the template, correlate with them in one matrix product. With the backbone fixed, our correlation remains essential while incurring negligible NPU overhead, offering a better speed-accuracy trade-off than the attention-based interactions we tested. TaCo-T achieves 62.3% LaSOT AUC at 184 FPS on the Rockchip RV1126B NPU and 96 FPS on a Raspberry Pi 5 CPU, 3.0x and 3.5x the NPU speed of HiT-Tiny and AsymTrack-T at higher accuracy. TaCo-B reaches 65.6% at 50 FPS on the NPU, more than twice the speed of HiT-Base, AsymTrack-B and LightFC-vit, again at higher accuracy. Code and models are available at https://github.com/TsingWei/TaCo-Tracker.

</details>


### [128] [From Diagnosing Alignment to Joint Supervision in Multimodal Draft Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34091)
*Haozhe Du,Runze Liu,Jiaming Fan,Yitong Tao,Shuxia Lin,Chonghan Liu,Daming Cao,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出通过分解草稿-目标对齐中的文本条件误差与视觉响应风险来诊断多模态草稿，并据此设计 MNRS-Joint 联合监督配比，在 LLaVA-1.5-7B 上提升投机解码的接受长度与加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 研究多模态草稿模型在同时接受多模态（MM）与纯文本（TXT）监督时的对齐问题，目标是确定如何组合两种监督以提升草稿-目标对齐质量，输出为联合监督配置方法及在投机解码上的接受长度与加速表现。

Motivation: 现有工作对 MM 与 TXT 两种监督各自如何影响草稿-目标对齐、以及如何组合二者尚不清楚；且仅凭聚合风险无法区分文本条件对齐相近但视觉响应风险与接受率显著不同的草稿。

Method: 通过对比有无视觉输入的成对预测，将分布误差分解为文本条件分量与视觉响应分量来诊断对齐；用受控续训发现高风险组优于随机与低风险组、视觉响应风险比聚合风险更能指导 MM 训练；据此提出 MNRS-Joint，依据草稿的分量风险分布，用模态特定的平均风险确定 MM:TXT 混合比例。

实验效果: 三次 warm-up 中其不同混合比例均落在比值扫描的高性能区域；在 LLaVA-1.5-7B 贪心树解码下，MNRS-Joint 相较同组成随机训练将平均接受长度由 3.35 提升至 3.53，平均加速比由 2.04× 提升至 2.16×，且随机链解码下增益依然成立。

Abstract: Multimodal drafts learn from multimodal (MM) and text-only (TXT) supervision, yet their effects on alignment and how to combine them remain unclear. We diagnose draft-target alignment through paired predictions with and without visual input, decomposing distributional error into text-conditioned and visual-response components. Drafts with similar text-conditioned alignment can still differ markedly in visual-response risk and acceptance. Controlled continuation training shows that high-risk groups outperform random and low-risk groups, and visual-response risk guides MM training more effectively than aggregate risk. Building on these diagnostics, we propose MNRS-Joint, which configures joint supervision from a given draft's component-risk distributions, using modality-specific mean risks to determine the MM:TXT mixture. Across three warm-ups, its distinct mixtures fall in high-performing regions of the ratio sweeps. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, MNRS-Joint improves mean accepted length from 3.35 to 3.53 and mean speedup from $2.04\times$ to $2.16\times$ over composition-matched random training under greedy tree decoding. The gains also hold under stochastic chain decoding.

</details>


### [129] [Exploring the Design Factors of Pixel-space Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34093)
*Mang Ning,Le Zhang,Albert Ali Salah,Itir Onal Ertugrul,Jintao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 EiT，从模型架构、表征与频域三个角度设计像素空间扩散 Transformer，在图像与视频生成上超越像素扩散与潜空间扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 本文研究像素空间（pixel-space）扩散模型的图像生成任务，并进一步扩展到视频像素扩散；输入为噪声，输出为生成的图像或视频，目标是提升像素空间扩散的生成质量。

Motivation: 现有像素扩散相对潜空间扩散在生成质量上仍不占优，其关键设计要素（架构、表征动态、频域行为）缺乏系统分析，论文据此识别可改进的设计方向。

Method: 受去噪自编码器（DAE）通过瓶颈去噪的启发，构建沿通道维度显式压缩的 U 形像素扩散 Transformer；分析发现像素与潜空间扩散 Transformer 均呈三阶段表征动态（去噪/局部特征扩展、基提升、后验投影），且更早去噪与更长基提升阶段有利于生成，通道瓶颈与加深网络可自然实现；同时证明在网络仍于像素空间运算时像素空间与频域扩散等价。

实验效果: 在 ImageNet 256×256 与 512×512 上 EiT 的 FID 分别为 1.61 与 1.73，优于 JiT 的 1.85 与 1.99；将 patch 扩展至时间维即可用于视频像素扩散，在 Kinetics-600（分辨率 256）上同等网络参数下 FVD 优于视频潜空间扩散。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce EiT by exploring the key design factors of pixel-space diffusion from three perspectives: model architecture, representation, and frequency domain. Inspired by Denoising Autoencoders (DAE) which perform denoising through a bottleneck, we construct a U-shaped pixel diffusion transformer that performs explicit compression along the channel dimension. In terms of representation, we find that both pixel and latent diffusion transformers exhibit a three-stage representation dynamics across layers and timesteps: denoising/local feature expansion, basis lift-up, and posterior projection. Moreover, earlier denoising and a longer basis lift-up stage are associated with better generation, which EiT naturally achieves through its channel bottleneck and increased network depth. From the frequency perspective, we establish the equivalence between pixel-space and frequency-space diffusion when keeping the network operating in pixel space. On ImageNet $256\times256$ and $512\times512$, EiT consistently outperforms JiT (Just image Transformers) in generation quality. Specifically, our EiT achieves better FID than JiT: 1.61 vs. 1.85 at $256\times256$, and 1.73 vs. 1.99 at $512\times512$. Remarkably, EiT can be seamlessly applied to video pixel diffusion by simply extending the patch size to temporal dimension and outperforms video latent diffusion in FVD evaluated on Kinetics-600 (resolution 256), under comparable total network parameters. Code is available at https://github.com/forever208/EiT

</details>


### [130] [FoMNet: Adaptive Kernels Meet Contextual Matching for Cross-Modal Homography Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34096)
*Zhuoran Yang,Zhenyu Yi,Runmin Zhang,Linshan Li,Yiming Li,Xiaokai Bai,Xiaohan Zhang,Hui-liang Shen,Si-Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FoMNet 通过学习式空间自适应融合 VFM 特征与上下文相关性细化，实现高精度跨模态单应性估计，并给出低延迟变体 FoMNet-D。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务为跨模态单应性估计：在成像模态外观差异显著的情况下，由跨模态图像对建立可靠对应关系，输出单应性变换矩阵；目标是提升多尺度细化框架下的配准精度。

Motivation: 多尺度细化虽能逐步缩小搜索范围，但其精度受限于匹配特征区分邻近候选的能力；视觉基础模型（VFM）特征虽有互补性，但现有方法以固定插值方式集成，使空间聚合既与图像内容无关，也与对应关系目标无关。

Method: 提出 FoMNet：Patch Feature Upsampling（PFU）在上采样网格上逐步预测位置特定的卷积核以聚合邻域特征，使 VFM 表示具备内容自适应的空间聚合能力，并在不同匹配尺度上与 CNN 特征融合；Contextual Correlation Refinement（CCR）联合处理源描述子与局部相关性及其空间上下文，在每次单应性更新前细化候选得分。另提出 FoMNet-D，用双三次插值替代 PFU，并借助多尺度特征关系蒸馏补偿精度损失以降低开销。

实验效果: 在五个跨模态数据集上，FoMNet 取得所评估方法中最低的平均平均角误差（MACE），相对 MCNet 降低 10.6%–72.7%；FoMNet-D 保持有竞争力的精度，同时相对 FoMNet 降低 34.0% 的推理延迟。

Abstract: Cross-modal homography estimation requires reliable correspondences despite appearance differences between sensing modalities. Multi-scale refinement progressively reduces the search range, but its accuracy remains limited by the ability of matching features to distinguish nearby candidates. The vision foundation model (VFM) provides complementary representations, yet integrating these features through fixed interpolation leaves their spatial aggregation independent of image content and the correspondence objective. We propose the Foundation Model-Enhanced Matching Network (FoMNet), which combines learned spatial adaptation of VFM features with contextual correspondence estimation. Patch Feature Upsampling (PFU) progressively predicts location-specific kernels to aggregate neighboring features on upsampled grids, preparing VFM representations for fusion with CNN features at different matching scales. Contextual Correlation Refinement (CCR) then jointly processes source descriptors and local correlations with spatial context to refine candidate scores before each homography update. To reduce upsampling overhead, we develop FoMNet-D, which replaces PFU with bicubic interpolation and uses multi-scale feature-relation distillation to recover accuracy. Experiments on five cross-modal datasets show that FoMNet achieves the lowest mean average corner error (MACE) among the evaluated methods, reducing MACE by 10.6%-72.7% relative to MCNet. FoMNet-D retains competitive accuracy while reducing inference latency by 34.0% relative to FoMNet.

</details>


### [131] [When Position Favors the Wrong Part: Evaluating Left-Right Discrimination in Semantic Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34100)
*Pingan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过构造“位置与部件冲突”条件，检验密集描述子在左右部件判别上的失效，并评估奇偶分量分数用于匹配坐标选择的实际效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是在同一目标图像及已标注部件对上评估部件判别与位置一致性，输入为密集描述子（含原始、水平反射下偶/奇分量），输出为判别准确率与匹配坐标选择规则的效果；目标是揭示总体判别准确率所掩盖的失败并检验分数能否指导匹配决策。

Motivation: 总体部件判别准确率会掩盖“图像位置偏向错误一侧部件”时的失败，现有工作缺乏在同一图像与同一标注部件对上对位置一致与冲突的对照评估，也未区分判别分数带来的修正与新增错误。

Method: 以边界框侧别分数定义一致/冲突条件，在同一批目标图像和标注部件对上比较位置一致性与冲突下的判别；比较原始描述子与其在水平反射下的偶、奇分量；在骨干与适配器冻结下检验特征上采样；并测试基于奇分数的候选选择规则，分别统计修正与引入的错误。

实验效果: SPair-71k 上，冻结 DINOv2 的奇判别准确率由一致时 86.66% 降至冲突时 19.63%，MARCO 为 95.27%→86.16%；固定 MARCO 输入几何时，框侧冲突下奇准确率在原生网格为 34.12%，经学习上采样并池化回该网格后达 88.84%。奇分数规则整体优于原始硬峰值匹配器（DINOv2、DINOv3）及 MARCO 冲突查询；MARCO 软解码器固定时重排余弦峰值使平均每对 PCK 提升 +0.47 pp；GeoAware-SC 用奇分数选定位窗口中心仅净增 15 个正确匹配（330 修正、315 新增错误）。

Abstract: Overall part-discrimination accuracy can conceal failures when image position favors the wrong bilateral part. We examine whether dense descriptors distinguish the correct part under this conflict and whether their discrimination scores help select matching coordinates. We evaluate position agreement and conflict within the same target images and annotated part pairs, using a bounding-box-side score to define the conditions. We compare the original descriptors with their even and odd components under horizontal reflection, examine feature upsampling with the backbone and adapters fixed, and test candidate-selection rules based on odd scores. On SPair-71k, odd discrimination accuracy falls from 86.66% under agreement to 19.63% under conflict for frozen DINOv2, compared with 95.27% to 86.16% for MARCO. With MARCO's input geometry fixed, odd accuracy in box-side conflict was 34.12% on the native grid and 88.84% after learned upsampling and pooling back to that grid. Odd-score rules improve on the original hard-peak matchers overall for DINOv2 and DINOv3 and on conflict queries for MARCO. With MARCO's soft decoder fixed, reranking the cosine peaks changes mean per-pair PCK by +0.47 pp. For supervised GeoAware-SC, using the odd score to select the localization window center yields only 15 net additional correct matches, with 330 corrections and 315 introduced errors. Evaluating discrimination under position conflict reveals failures hidden by aggregate accuracy. Reporting corrections and introduced errors separately also shows whether a discriminative score produces useful matching decisions.

</details>


### [132] [What Makes a Gavagai? Learning Visual Concepts from One Positive and Few Negatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34105)
*Katsuya Ogata,Kazuya Nishimura,Yuta Nakashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 受 Quine"gavagai"问题启发，作者提出 Gavagai Benchmark，用正负样例检验视觉语言模型能否从少量观察中识别界定新概念的关键视觉属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务要求模型在给定新概念的正例与负例图像后，识别出定义该概念的视觉属性，数据包括合成的新属性组合图像与新近描述物种的真实图像，目标是评估模型的少样本概念归纳能力。

Motivation: 单一观察可与多个概念相容，仅靠准确的视觉感知不足以确定目标解释；现有模型是否以及如何利用负例排除竞争假设、从而消除这种歧义，尚缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 构建 Gavagai Benchmark（合成属性组合图像 + 新物种真实图像），让模型从正负样例中归纳概念；通过对比"给定正确属性"与"用模型预测属性替代真值"两种诊断设置，分别剥离并分析视觉感知与推理两类能力的作用。

实验效果: 抽象未报告具体数值结果；定性发现为：部分模型即使在提供正确属性时仍表现困难，另一些模型在真值属性被模型预测替代后出现显著性能落差，说明属性感知与有限证据下的推理均存在挑战。

Abstract: Humans can recognize novel concepts from only a few observations by identifying defining visual attributes and eliminating alternative interpretations. However, a single observation is consistent with multiple possible concepts, and accurate visual perception alone is insufficient to determine the intended interpretation. Negative evidence can reduce this ambiguity by eliminating competing hypotheses. Inspired by Quine's gavagai problem, we introduce the Gavagai Benchmark to evaluate whether vision-language models can identify the attributes defining a novel concept from positive and negative examples. The benchmark consists of synthetic images depicting novel combinations of visual attributes and real images of newly described species. Using these datasets, we examine how models use negative examples to identify novel concepts and analyze the respective roles of visual perception and reasoning. Several models struggle even when correct attributes are provided, while others show large gaps when ground-truth attributes are replaced with model predictions. These diagnostic comparisons highlight challenges in both attribute perception and inference from limited evidence.

</details>


### [133] [Detectable by Design: Learning a Continuous 3D Saliency Field from 2D Detector Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34108)
*Han Sun,Jun Li,Christy Jie Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出以"图像可检测性"定义3D显著性，并引入连续场模型DetField，从点云预测任意空间位置的可检测性以提升稀疏地图下的相机定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是从点云中选择用于相机定位的3D点：地图中保留的3D点决定相机能否被定位，地图越稀疏该选择越关键。输入为点云，输出为可指导选点的3D显著性。

Motivation: 现有3D关键点检测器以语义、可重复性、重建质量或描述子可匹配性定义显著性，均未考虑该点是否会在图像中被真正检测到，导致所选点大多无法在图像中再次被检测。

Method: 将3D显著性定义为2D兴趣点检测器在表面点处、在其全部可见视图上的响应聚合；用2D检测器响应从图像反投影到点云作为监督，训练DetField这一以点云为输入、可预测任意位置可检测性的连续场；并在每个查询点将多尺度曲率不变量直接送入解码器，绕过编码器的体素池化。

实验效果: 在隔离3D选点选择的定位基准上，DetField在物体与真实室内场景上优于Harris-3D及五种学习式3D关键点方法；在未见房间上定位成功率较最强基线Harris-3D提升7.4个点，在未参与训练的2D检测器下于物体上同样领先，且在稀疏地图上于物体和真实场景均保持领先。

Abstract: The 3D points kept in a map decide whether a camera can be localized against it, and this choice matters more as the map becomes sparser. Existing 3D keypoint detectors usually define saliency by semantics, repeatability, reconstruction or descriptor matchability. None of these definitions asks whether a point will actually be detected in an image, and as a result most of the points they select are not detected again in images.
  In this paper, we define 3D saliency as image detectability: the response of a 2D interest-point detector at a surface point, aggregated over all views in which the point is visible. We propose DetField, a continuous field over 3D space that takes a point cloud as input and predicts detectability at any location around it. The supervision is the response of a 2D detector, back-projected from images onto the point cloud. Multi-scale curvature invariants are passed to the decoder at every query point, bypassing the voxel pooling of the encoder.
  In a localization benchmark that isolates the choice of 3D points, DetField outperforms Harris-3D and five learned 3D keypoint methods on objects and on real indoor scenes. On unseen rooms, it improves localization success by 7.4 points over Harris-3D, the strongest baseline. On objects, it also leads under a 2D detector not used in training. On sparse maps, it stays ahead of Harris-3D on both objects and real scenes.

</details>


### [134] [A Simple Yet Effective Agent for Open-World Aerial Object Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34115)
*Jitai Hao,Liangxing Yang,Zhenyang Li,Fan Liu,Qiang Huang,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimpleUAV 提出一种基于冻结现成视觉语言模型、无需训练和持久地图的极简空中目标搜索智能体，在多个基准上显著超越已有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是开放世界空中目标搜索：智能体在航空场景中依据视觉观测决定移动，以找到指定目标，输出为由局部几何约束的连续运动决策与执行反馈。目标是检验极简、免训练设计能达到的上限，并为后续引入架构复杂性提供对照基线。

Motivation: 现有方法依赖持久空间地图、学习得到的世界模型或任务特定策略训练，架构与训练成本较高，但尚不清楚这些复杂性是否必要，缺乏对极简免训练方案能力边界的系统检验。

Method: SimpleUAV 以冻结的现成 VLM 为核心，将当前视角的目标评估与基于历史的动作选择解耦：前者提供最新的候选证据，后者结合轻量搜索上下文进行决策；再由局部几何把视觉选择转换为有界直线移动，用深度检查中止被阻挡的航段，并以实测执行反馈引导后续决策，形成无需持久空间地图与任务特定训练的闭环搜索。

实验效果: 在 UAV-ON 上成功率达 41.61%（最强基线 23.80%），在 AerialDojo-200K 上为 11.98%（基线 3.20%）；经少量基准特定适配后，同一核心智能体在 TravelUAV 的空中 VLN 任务上于 L1 辅助下三个数据划分的 Full SR 均至少提升 19.66%。

Abstract: Open-world aerial object search methods often rely on persistent spatial maps, learned world models, or task-specific policy training. To examine how far a minimal, training-free design can go, we introduce SimpleUAV, an agent built around a frozen, off-the-shelf vision-language model (VLM). SimpleUAV separates current-view target assessment from history-conditioned action selection, combining fresh candidate evidence with lightweight search context. Local geometry converts visual selections into bounded straight-line movements, depth-based checks halt blocked segments, and measured execution feedback guides subsequent decisions. This closed loop sustains search without persistent spatial maps or task-specific training. SimpleUAV achieves 41.61% Success Rate (SR) on UAV-ON and 11.98% on AerialDojo-200K, compared with 23.80% and 3.20% for the strongest prior baselines, respectively. With limited benchmark-specific adaptation, the same core agent transfers to aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) on TravelUAV, improving Full SR by at least 19.66% across all three splits under L1 assistance. These results establish a strong baseline for evaluating when additional architectural complexity benefits aerial search.

</details>


### [135] [Out of Sight, Not Out of Clues: Can Video LLMs Guess from Partial Evidence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34126)
*Luoxi Zhang,Jiawei Liu,Yanlin Luo,Peiyang He,Junlin Zhu,Chun To Choi,Yang Xiong,Jiang Wu,Xingyu Zeng,Min Yang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出诊断基准 ClueProbe，将 Video LLM 的证据感知与充分性判断分离，揭示失败源于判断而非感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 在自我中心录像、视觉无障碍、监控回看等场景中，Video LLM 往往只能获得部分视觉证据；模型应在证据充足时作答、部分可见时基于线索推测、证据缺失时拒答。本文构建基准以刻画并评测这种分级充分性感知能力。

Motivation: 现有基准仅采用“可答/不可答”二元划分，未衡量部分证据情形，也无法区分失败原因是缺失证据还是误判充分性，导致模型行为与证据支持程度不匹配的问题被掩盖。

Method: 构建 ClueProbe：598 个自然视频、1,721 个人工标注问题，标注为 Answerable / Guessable / Unanswerable 三级，并配有改写措辞或改变视觉输入的受控扰动，用于分离感知与充分性判断；再通过针对性微调（监督 guess 层级）验证诊断，并与常规拒答微调对比。

实验效果: 评测 10 个开源与专有 Video LLM 表明失败源于判断而非感知；监督 guess 层级可将恰当推测从 1.6% 提升至 40.4% 且不增加错误拒答，而常规拒答微调会拒绝 92% 的可推测问题。

Abstract: In real-world applications like egocentric recording, visual accessibility, and surveillance review, visual evidence for Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) is often only partially captured. A reliable model should match its response to the evidence: answering when sufficient, guessing from visible clues when partial, and abstaining when absent. Yet existing benchmarks enforce a binary answerable-or-unanswerable split, leaving partial evidence unmeasured and obscuring whether failures arise from missing evidence or misjudging sufficiency. We introduce ClueProbe, a diagnostic benchmark to (1) separate perceiving evidence from judging sufficiency, and (2) validate our diagnosis via targeted fine-tuning. ClueProbe comprises 1,721 human-annotated questions across 598 natural videos, labeled Answerable, Guessable, or Unanswerable, alongside controlled perturbations altering wording or visual input. Evaluating 10 open-source and proprietary Video LLMs reveals that failures stem from judgment rather than perception: models perceive the evidence yet lack graded sufficiency awareness, answering or refusing outright while rarely guessing from partial clues. Targeted fine-tuning shows that this reflects missing supervision rather than insufficient capacity: supervising the guess level lifts appropriate guessing from 1.6% to 40.4% without extra false refusals, whereas conventional refusal tuning rejects 92% of guessable questions. Data and code will be released at https://github.com/suat-llm/ClueProbe.

</details>


### [136] [FoundSurface: Feedforward Ray-Based 3D Scene Surface Reconstruction from Unposed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34128)
*Shenxing Wei,Jinxi Li,Zihui Zhang,Ajay Kumar,Wei Wang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出融合pointmap与ray表示的通用前馈式管线，从无位姿图像直接重建连续高保真3D表面，并具备强零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务是从无相机位姿的图像中精确重建3D表面，是计算机视觉的基础难题；输入为未标定位姿的多视图图像，输出为连续、高精度的3D表面几何，目标是摆脱对位姿的依赖并获得可泛化的重建能力。

Motivation: 现有前馈式pointmap方法虽免去相机位姿需求，但本质输出离散、稀疏的表示，无法直接得到连续高保真的表面，因而需要新的表示融合方案。

Method: 先用预训练pointmap模型提取显式几何先验；再由以查询视角为中心的选择模块，借助鲁棒的surfel投票机制挑选几何相关的参考视图；随后用交叉注意力回归器将目标查询射线与参考特征关联，估计连续粗糙表面；最后由raylet模块聚合局部3D特征以刻画高频细节并解析法向。

实验效果: 在9个数据集上作为单一基础模型训练，在6个未见数据集上展现优异零样本泛化，摘要称其在几何精度与细节保持上明显优于最先进基线，但未给出具体数值指标。

Abstract: Accurate 3D surface reconstruction from unposed images remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. Recent feed-forward pointmap methods eliminate the need for camera poses but inherently produce discrete, sparse representations. In this paper, we present a generalizable pipeline that directly recovers continuous, high-fidelity 3D surfaces from unposed images by seamlessly integrating pointmap- and ray-based representations. Our approach first extracts explicit geometric priors using a pre-trained pointmap model. A query-view-centric selection module then identifies geometrically relevant reference views via a robust surfel voting mechanism. Next, a cross-attention regressor bridges target query rays with reference features to estimate a continuous coarse surface. Lastly, a raylet-based module aggregates local 3D features to carve out high-frequency details and analytical normals. Trained as a single foundation model on 9 diverse datasets, our method demonstrates exceptional zero-shot generalization on 6 unseen datasets, clearly surpassing state-of-the-art baselines in geometric accuracy and detail preservation.

</details>


### [137] [A Calibration-Referenced Protocol for Measuring Decision Stability under Reference Bank Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34134)
*Yiwen Chen,Shengming Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套四步评测协议，在匹配部署规模下比较参考库压缩方法对异常判决的影响，发现10%预算下的乘积量化（PQ）在决策不一致度上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 工业图像检测中，参考库需压缩以适配部署预算，但压缩后的异常分数与工作阈值共同决定最终判决。论文以VisA和MVTec AD为输入基准，输出一种将部署规模与以校准为参照的决策度量相联系的评测协议，目标是评估压缩方法在真实判决层面的影响。

Motivation: 现有工作多只报告压缩后的分数层面指标（如AUROC），未在同等的部署规模下比较方法，也未考察压缩分数与工作阈值耦合后产生的判决偏移；缺少以校准为参照、能定位受影响样本群体的决策级评测手段。

Method: 协议分四步：一是在匹配的部署规模下比较各压缩方法；二是由200次重校准建立参考分布；三是针对两个校准基准评估判决不一致度；四是按样本距全库阈值的距离度量变化。比较对象包括预算匹配的PQ与int8标量量化等五种方法，并在ResNet-18、干净参考库、正常目录探针估计阈值等设定下复现。

实验效果: 在WideResNet-50、10%预算下，PQ在VisA和MVTec AD两个工作水平上的判决不一致度最低，其余四个预算下int8标量量化更优；MVTec AD较低工作水平下PQ在34/39单元达到校准中位数、39/39达到上分位，图像级AUROC损失为0.0003–0.0037。VisA在1%预算与1%工作水平下，近阈值异常样本的不一致度达28.12%（95% CI 23.25–33.58%），正常留出集为1.30%（0.79–2.14%）；10% PQ设定在ResNet-18、干净参考库及正常目录阈值估计下均保持最低不一致度。

Abstract: Reference banks used for industrial image inspection can be compressed to fit a deployment budget, but the resulting anomaly decisions depend on both the compressed scores and the operating threshold. We present a four-step protocol that compares compression methods at matched deployment sizes, establishes a reference distribution from 200 recalibrations, evaluates disagreement against two calibration benchmarks, and measures changes by distance from the full-bank threshold. With WideResNet-50, budget-matched product quantization (PQ) at the 10% budget has the lowest decision disagreement among five matched methods, including int8 scalar quantization, on VisA and MVTec AD at both operating levels. Int8 scalar quantization gives lower disagreement than PQ at the other four budgets. On MVTec AD at the lower operating level, PQ meets the calibration median in 34/39 cells and the upper quantile in 39/39 cells. Category-equally weighted image-level AUROC loss at this budget ranges from 0.0003 to 0.0037 across the evaluated configurations. On VisA at the 1% budget and a 1% operating level, disagreement reaches 28.12% (95% CI 23.25--33.58%) among near-threshold anomalies and 1.30% (0.79--2.14%) across the normal holdout. The 10% budget-matched PQ setting retains the lowest disagreement among four compared methods with ResNet-18, with clean reference banks, and when thresholds are estimated from normal-directory probes. The protocol connects deployment size to a calibration-referenced decision measure and identifies the evaluation populations most informative for inspection.

</details>


### [138] [An Image Detectability Field as a Viewpoint Utility for 2D-3D Localization in Sparse Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34139)
*Han Sun,Jun Li,Christy Jie Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文探究以「图像可检测性场」作为2D-3D定位中的视角效用函数是否有用，并在合成与真实场景中与计数、Harris-3D等基线对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 在2D-3D定位中，相机通过将图像与3D地图匹配来估计自身位姿；手持平或机器人搭载的相机在拍摄前常可自主选择视角。本文的输入是地图几何（近表面3D点）与候选视角，输出是被选视角下的位姿估计，目标是判断可检测性场能否作为有效的视角选择依据。

Motivation: 现有视角规划器主要依赖地图几何（如可见地标计数、Harris-3D、曲率）来度量视角效用，而未考虑2D检测器在该视角下是否真的会响应这些地标点，可能导致规划器高估某些视角的可定位性。

Method: 构建可在表面附近任意3D点查询的「图像可检测性场」：由点的局部几何预测2D检测器在该点投影处响应的概率；直接使用已训练好的场、不为视角选择做微调。将场对可见地标求和作为视角效用，配以固定的SuperPoint与EPnP-RANSAC定位器；在真实场景中把它加入学习式逐地标规划器，用于地标选择与视角评分。

实验效果: 合成ShapeNet稀疏点图上，场求和比地标计数高+2.0个百分点定位成功率（95%区间[+0.7,+3.2]），比Harris-3D秩求和高+2.2[+0.8,+3.5]；但在SceneFun3D真实房间上，其学习式视角评分器LWL的改版在测试房间低于计数，故改用逐地标规划器：稀疏地图下带场版本比不带场高+1.8点，比替换为Harris-3D或曲率高+1.0与+0.8点，三个区间均高于零；两种设置下该场在稀疏地图中有效，在稠密地图中与Harris-3D相当。

Abstract: In 2D-3D localization, a camera estimates its pose by matching an image to a 3D map. A camera held in a hand or mounted on a robot can often choose its viewpoint before it takes the image. In this work, we ask whether an image detectability field is a useful viewpoint utility when the planner reads only the geometry of the map. The field can be queried at any 3D point near a surface. From the local geometry around the point, it predicts how likely a 2D image detector is to fire at the projection of the point. We use the trained field as it is, without fine-tuning it for view selection. A fixed SuperPoint and EPnP-RANSAC localizer then estimates the pose from the chosen view. We answer in two settings, each with a test registered before the runs. On synthetic ShapeNet objects with sparse point maps, summing the field over the visible landmarks beats counting them by +2.0 points of localization success rate (object-clustered 95% interval [+0.7, +3.2]). It also beats summing Harris-3D ranks by +2.2 [+0.8, +3.5]. On real indoor scenes from SceneFun3D, our adaptation of the learned view scorer LWL falls below counting on the test rooms. We therefore add the field to a learned per-landmark planner. On the test rooms with sparse maps whose landmarks the field also selects, the planner with the field beats the same planner without it by +1.8 points. It beats the planner with Harris-3D or curvature in its place by +1.0 and +0.8 points. All three room-clustered intervals lie above zero. In both settings, the field helps in sparse maps and is on par with Harris-3D in dense maps.

</details>


### [139] [Benefit of Structured Representations: When Equivariance Helps in Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34140)
*Florian A. Hölzl,Daniel Rueckert,Georgios Kaissis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: The paper empirically shows that an equivariant ConvNeXt variant (ConvNeXt-C4) achieves better accuracy with substantially fewer parameters and FLOPs, and that its advantage grows as training data shrinks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: The paper studies when vision encoders benefit from geometric inductive biases, using image classification as a controlled invariant objective and the discrete quarter-turn rotation group (C4) as the symmetry, with inputs of images and outputs of class labels.

Motivation: Mainstream vision encoders rarely incorporate equivariance for most geometric symmetries despite the promise of structural guarantees, and it remains unclear when such biases actually help; C4 on images allows a lossless study without interpolation or boundary artifacts.

Method: The authors introduce ConvNeXt-C4, a rotation-equivariant ConvNeXt with dimension-equivalent yet orientation-structured representations, and train it from scratch, comparing it to the base model across tasks and dataset sizes, and analyzing the classifier head's use of orientation information.

实验效果: Trained from scratch, ConvNeXt-C4 outperforms its base model on all tasks while using 6x fewer parameters and 1.6x fewer FLOPs; its advantage grows log-linearly as training set size decreases, pre-training helps both variants similarly, and exploiting orientation structure in the classifier head improves performance by 4.90% across all datasets.

Abstract: Equivariant neural networks promise improved representation learning through structural guarantees. However, mainstream vision encoders rarely incorporate equivariance for most geometric symmetries. Images are a particularly interesting modality with many such symmetries and extensive datasets from which to learn this structure. In this work, we investigate when vision encoders benefit from geometric inductive biases. We study the discrete rotation group of quarter-turns (C4) which acts losslessly without interpolation or boundary artifacts. Combined with image classification as a controlled invariant objective, this allows for a principled empirical investigation into the role of structural guarantees. We introduce ConvNeXt-C4 as a rotation equivariant ConvNeXt with dimension-equivalent yet orientation-structured representations. Training from scratch, our experiments show, that the C4 variant outperforms its base model across all tasks while operating with 6x fewer parameters and 1.6x fewer FLOPs.
